## Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST) Regulations {#selected-listed-financial-institutions-attribution-method-gsthst-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2001-171
:::

[EXCISE TAX ACT](/eng/acts/E-15)

Registration 2001-05-10

Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
Regulations

P.C. 2001-828  2001-05-10
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Finance, pursuant to paragraph 225.2(1)(d)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0E2FAA}, paragraph
(a)[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EZFAA}
of the description of A in subsection 225.2(2), the description of
C[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EXFAA} in
that subsection, paragraph (a)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EVFAA} of the
description of F in that subsection, the description of G[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0ETFAA} in that
subsection, subsection 228(2.2)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0ERFAA}, the
description of D[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e){#footnotec_e-ID0EPFAA} in
subparagraph 237(5)(b)(ii), section 277[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#footnoted_e){#footnoted_e-ID0ENFAA}, the
description of D[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#footnotee_e){#footnotee_e-ID0ELFAA} in
subparagraph 363(2)(a)(ii), the description of D[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#footnotee_e){#footnotee_e-ID0EJFAA} in paragraph
363(2)(b), the description of F[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#footnotee_e){#footnotee_e-ID0EHFAA} in
subparagraph 363(2)(c)(ii) and the description of F[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#footnotee_e){#footnotee_e-ID0EFFAA} in paragraph
363(2)(d) of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), hereby makes the
annexed [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution Method
(GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171).

\]

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Excise Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/E-15). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[defined benefits pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[defined benefits pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      part of a pension plan that is in respect of benefits under the
      plan that are determined in accordance with a formula set forth in
      the plan and under which the employer contributions are not
      determined in accordance with a formula set forth in the
      plan. ([régime de pension à prestations
      déterminées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[defined contribution pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[defined contribution pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the part of a pension plan that is not a defined benefits pension
      plan. ([régime de pension à cotisations
      déterminées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributed investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributed investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      investment plan that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a corporation (other than a pension entity)
        exempt from tax under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) by
        reason of paragraph 149(1)(o.2) of that Act;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an investment corporation;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a mortgage investment corporation;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a mutual fund corporation;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a mutual fund trust;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a non-resident-owned investment corporation;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a segregated fund of an insurer;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a unit trust that is not a trust described in
        any of subparagraphs 149(5)(a)(i) to (ix) and (xiii) of the Act.

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an investment limited partnership. ([régime de
        placement par répartition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[employer resource]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employer resource]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 172.1(1) of the Act. ([ressource
      d'employeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[exchange-traded fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[exchange-traded fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributed
      investment plan, any unit of which is listed or traded on a stock
      exchange or other public market. ([fonds coté en
      bourse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[exchange-traded series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[exchange-traded series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a stratified
      investment plan means a series of the plan, any unit of which is
      listed or traded on a stock exchange or other public
      market. ([série cotée en bourse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the estate or
      succession of a deceased
      individual. ([particulier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person referred to
      in subparagraph 149(1)(a)(vi) or (ix) of the Act, other than

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a trust governed by a registered retirement
        savings plan, a registered retirement income fund, a registered
        disability savings plan or a TFSA; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trust governed by a registered education
        savings plan if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the trust does not have more than one
          beneficiary at any one time, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each of the beneficiaries of the trust is
          connected to each living subscriber under the plan, or was
          connected to a deceased original subscriber under the plan, by
          blood relationship or adoption (within the meaning of
          subsection 251(6) of the [Income Tax
          Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)). ([régime de
          placement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an investment plan means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pension entity of a registered
        pension plan, the administrator, as defined in subsection
        147.1(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), of the
        pension plan;

      - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pension entity of a pooled
        registered pension plan, the PRPP administrator of the pension
        plan; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the person that has ultimate
        responsibility for the management and administration of the
        assets and liabilities of the investment
        plan. ([gestionnaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      distributed investment plan that is not a stratified investment
      plan. ([régime de placement non stratifié]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[permanent establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permanent establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person means
      any permanent establishment that the person is deemed to have
      under section 3 and

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation other than an
        investment plan, any permanent establishment of the corporation
        as determined under subsection 400(2) of the [Income Tax
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an individual or a trust other
        than an investment plan, any permanent establishment of the
        individual or trust as determined under subsection 2600(2) of
        the [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945);
        and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a partnership that is not an
        investment plan,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if all the members of the partnership are
          individuals or trusts, any permanent establishment that would
          be a permanent establishment of the partnership under
          subsection 2600(2) of the [Income Tax
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945) if the
          partnership were an individual, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if subparagraph (i) does not apply, any
          permanent establishment that would be a permanent
          establishment of the partnership under subsection 400(2) of
          the [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945)
          if the partnership were a corporation. ([établissement
          stable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 27, s. 55\]]{.Repealed}

  [[plan member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plan member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an investment plan that is
      a private investment plan or a pension entity of a pension plan
      means an individual who has a right, either immediate or in the
      future and either absolute or contingent, to receive benefits
      under

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an employee life and health
        trust, the investment plan;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pension entity of a pension
        plan, the pension plan; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the deferred profit sharing
        plan, the employee benefit plan, the employee trust, the
        employees profit sharing plan, the registered education savings
        plan, the registered supplementary unemployment benefit plan or
        the retirement compensation arrangement, as the case may be,
        that governs the investment
        plan. ([participant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[private investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[private investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      investment plan other than

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a distributed investment plan; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pension entity. ([régime de placement
        privé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for a fiscal year of a
      stratified investment plan means a series of the stratified
      investment plan that meets the following conditions throughout the
      fiscal year in respect of a particular province:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} under the laws of Canada or a province, units
        of the series are permitted to be sold or distributed in the
        particular province and are not permitted to be sold or
        distributed in any other province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} under the terms of the prospectus, registration
        statement, partnership agreement or other similar document for
        the series, or under the laws of Canada or a province, the
        conditions for a person owning or acquiring units of the series
        include the following:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the person be resident in the particular
          province when the units are acquired, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that the units are required to be sold,
          transferred or redeemed within a reasonable time after the
          person ceases to be resident in the particular province; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the stratified investment plan's percentage for
        the series, for the particular province and for the taxation
        year in which the preceding fiscal year ends, or the percentage
        that would be the stratified investment plan's percentage for
        the series, for the particular province and for that taxation
        year if the particular province were a participating province,
        is 90% or more. ([série provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a trust, a class of units of the
        trust;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a corporation,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a class of the capital stock of the
          corporation that has not been issued in one or more series,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a series of a class of the capital stock of
          the corporation that has been issued in one or more series;
          and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a partnership, a class of units
        of the partnership. ([série]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified resource]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified resource]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 172.1(1) of the Act. ([ressource
      déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      distributed investment plan whose units are issued in two or more
      series. ([régime de placement stratifié]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[subscriber]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subscriber]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} under a registered education
      savings plan has the same meaning as in subsection 146.1(1) of the
      [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([souscripteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a trust, a unit of the trust;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a series of a trust, a unit of
        the trust of that series;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a corporation, a share of the
        capital stock of the corporation;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a series of a corporation, a
        share of the capital stock of the corporation of that series;

      - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a partnership, an interest of a
        person in the partnership;

      - [(d.2)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a series of a partnership, a
        unit of the partnership of that series; and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a segregated fund of an insurer,
        an interest of a person, other than the insurer, in the
        segregated fund. ([unité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further definitions --- [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, [[deferred
  profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[employee benefit
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[employee life and health
  trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[employee trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
  [[employees profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[investment
  corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mortgage investment
  corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mutual fund
  corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mutual fund
  trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[non-resident-owned investment
  corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[registered disability savings
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[registered education savings
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[registered retirement income
  fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[registered retirement savings
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[registered supplementary unemployment
  benefit plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[retirement compensation
  arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[TFSA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[unit trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings as in
  subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of definitions to
  adaptations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the definitions in this
  section apply in subsection 225.2(2) of the Act as adapted by these
  Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying partnership

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, a partnership that is not an investment plan is a
qualifying partnership during a taxation year of the partnership if, at
any time in the taxation year, the partnership has

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member that has, at any time in the taxation year
  of the member in which the taxation year of the partnership ends, a
  permanent establishment in a particular participating province through
  which a business of the partnership is carried on or that is deemed
  under section 3 to be a permanent establishment of the member; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a member (including a member referred to in paragraph
  (a)) that has, at any time in the taxation year of the member in which
  the taxation year of the partnership ends, a permanent establishment
  in a province other than the particular participating province through
  which a business of the partnership is carried on or that is deemed
  under section 3 to be a permanent establishment of the member.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
- 2018, c. 27, s. 56
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2-20130418.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishment in province

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is a bank or a credit
  union and if, at any time in a taxation year of the financial
  institution, the financial institution maintains a deposit or other
  similar account that is in the name of a person resident in a province
  or, at any time in that year, a loan that was made by the financial
  institution is outstanding and is secured by land situated in a
  province or, if not secured by land, is owing by a person resident in
  a province, the following rules apply:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the financial institution is deemed to have a
    permanent establishment in the province throughout the taxation
    year, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the following loans made by the financial
    institution and deposit and other similar accounts maintained by the
    financial institution are deemed to be loans and deposits of the
    permanent establishment referred to in subparagraph (i) and not of
    any other permanent establishment of the financial institution:

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} outstanding loans secured by land situated in the
      province,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} outstanding loans, not secured by land, owing by
      persons resident in the province, and

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} deposit and other similar accounts in the name of
      a person resident in the province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is an insurer that, at any
  time in a taxation year of the financial institution, is insuring a
  risk in respect of property ordinarily situated in a province or in
  respect of a person resident in a province, the financial institution
  is deemed to have a permanent establishment in the province throughout
  the taxation year;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is a trust and loan
  corporation, a trust corporation or a loan corporation and if, at any
  time in a taxation year of the financial institution, the financial
  institution conducts business (other than business in respect of
  loans) in a province or, at any time in that year, a loan that was
  made by the financial institution is outstanding and is secured by
  land situated in a province or, if not secured by land, is owing by a
  person resident in a province, the financial institution is deemed to
  have a permanent establishment in the province throughout the taxation
  year;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is a segregated fund of an
  insurer, the financial institution is deemed to have a permanent
  establishment in a particular province throughout a taxation year of
  the financial institution if, at any time in the taxation year,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the insurer is qualified, under the laws of Canada
    or a province, to sell units of the financial institution in the
    particular province, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person resident in the particular province holds
    one or more units of the financial institution;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is a distributed
  investment plan (other than a segregated fund of an insurer), the
  financial institution is deemed to have a permanent establishment in a
  particular province throughout a taxation year of the financial
  institution if, at any time in the taxation year,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the financial institution is qualified, under the
    laws of Canada or a province, to sell or distribute units of the
    financial institution in the particular province, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person resident in the particular province holds
    one or more units of the financial institution; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if a financial institution is a private investment
  plan or an investment plan that is a pension entity of a pension plan
  and, at any time in a taxation year of the financial institution, a
  plan member of the financial institution is resident in a province,
  the financial institution is deemed to have a permanent establishment
  in the province throughout the taxation year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishment throughout
taxation year

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, a financial institution has a permanent establishment in a
province throughout a taxation year of the financial institution if the
financial institution has a permanent establishment in the province at
any time in the taxation year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Residence of person

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, and despite subsection 132.1(1) of the Act, a person
resident in Canada is resident in the province

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is an individual, where the person's
  principal mailing address in Canada is located;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a corporation or a partnership,
  where the person's principal business in Canada is located;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a trust governed by a registered
  retirement savings plan, a registered retirement income fund, a
  registered education savings plan, a registered disability savings
  plan or a TFSA, where the principal mailing address in Canada of the
  annuitant of the registered retirement savings plan or registered
  retirement income fund, of the subscriber of the registered education
  savings plan or of the holder of the registered disability savings
  plan or TFSA is located;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a trust, other than a trust
  described in paragraph (c), where the trustee's principal business in
  Canada is located or, if the trustee is not carrying on a business,
  where the trustee's principal mailing address in Canada is located;
  and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, where the person's principal
  business in Canada is located or, if the person is not carrying on a
  business, where the person's principal mailing address in Canada is
  located.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- section 225.3 of Act

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purpose of section 225.3 of the Act, [[exchange-traded
  fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[exchange-traded
  series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[non-stratified investment
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[stratified investment
  plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings as in subsection
  1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- section 225.4 of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of section 225.4 of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[exchange-traded fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
    [[exchange-traded series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
    [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[investment
    plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[non-stratified investment
    plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[plan member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
    [[private investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
    [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[stratified investment
    plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the
    same meanings as in subsection 1(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[specified investor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
    same meaning as in section 16.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Prescribed Financial Institutions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-667393 .Part}

Meaning of [[unrecoverable tax amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this section, [[unrecoverable tax
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for a reporting period of a person means
  the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#667397 .Subsection}
  A -- B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount that would be included in the total
        for A in subsection 225.2(2) of the Act, read without reference
        to any adaptation made under Part 5, for the reporting period,
        if the person were a selected listed financial institution
        throughout the reporting period and no election under subsection
        55(1) were in effect throughout the reporting period,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amount of tax that the person would be
        deemed to have paid under any of subsections 172.1(5) to (7.1)
        of the Act during the reporting period, if the person were a
        selected listed financial institution throughout the reporting
        period, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an amount that the person would be required by
        paragraph 232.01(5)(b) or 232.02(4)(b) of the Act to include in
        its determination of net tax for the reporting period, if the
        person were a selected listed financial institution throughout
        the reporting period; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount that would be included in the total
        for B in subsection 225.2(2) of the Act, read without reference
        to any adaptation made under Part 5, for the reporting period,
        if the person were a selected listed financial institution
        throughout the reporting period and no election under subsection
        55(1) were in effect throughout the reporting period,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the federal component amount, within the
        meaning of section 232.01 of the Act, of a tax adjustment note
        issued under subsection 232.01(3) of the Act to the person
        during the reporting period, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the federal component amount, within the
        meaning of section 232.02 of the Act, of a tax adjustment note
        issued under subsection 232.02(2) of the Act to the person
        during the reporting period.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying small investment plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, an investment plan
  (other than a distributed investment plan) is a qualifying small
  investment plan for a particular fiscal year of the investment plan
  where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, in the absence of section 57, the particular
    fiscal year is the first fiscal year of the investment plan, the
    amount determined by the following formula for each reporting period
    of the investment plan included in the particular fiscal year is
    equal to or less than \$10,000:

    ::: {#667410 .Paragraph}
    A × (365/B)

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the unrecoverable tax amount for the reporting period, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the reporting period; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount determined by the
    following formula is equal to or less than \$10,000:

    ::: {#667415 .Paragraph}
    A × (365/B)

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an unrecoverable
        tax amount for a reporting period of the investment plan
        included in the fiscal year of the investment plan (in this
        paragraph referred to as the "preceding fiscal year") that
        precedes the particular fiscal year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the preceding fiscal year.
    :::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-162, s. 9(F)
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 15](#1115666-1116838){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115666-1116838.html"}
:::

:::

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-59,
s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

Version](section-8-20130418.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed financial institution ---
paragraph 225.2(1)(b) of Act

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 10 to
15 and for the purpose of paragraph 225.2(1)(b) of the Act, a financial
institution is a prescribed financial institution throughout a reporting
period in a particular fiscal year that ends in a taxation year of the
financial institution if the financial institution

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has, at any time in the taxation year, a permanent
  establishment in a participating province and has, at any time in the
  taxation year, a permanent establishment in any other province; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a qualifying partnership during the taxation year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-162, s. 11(F)
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20060623.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- qualifying small investment
plans

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** Section 9 does not apply
in respect of a reporting period in a particular fiscal year of a
financial institution that is a qualifying small investment plan for the
particular fiscal year if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the financial institution was a qualifying small
  investment plan for the fiscal year of the financial institution that
  precedes the particular fiscal year and was not a selected listed
  financial institution throughout that preceding fiscal year;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the financial institution was a selected listed
  financial institution throughout the three fiscal years of the
  financial institution that precede the particular fiscal year; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the particular fiscal year is the first fiscal year
  of the financial institution.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- provincial investment plan

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** Section 9 does not apply
in respect of a reporting period in a fiscal year that ends in a
taxation year of a financial institution that is a non-stratified
investment plan and that meets the following conditions throughout the
fiscal year in respect of a particular province:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} under the laws of Canada or a province, units of the
  financial institution are permitted to be sold or distributed in the
  particular province but are not permitted to be sold or distributed in
  any other province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} under the terms of the prospectus, registration
  statement, partnership agreement or other similar document for the
  financial institution, or under the laws of Canada or a province, the
  conditions for a person owning or acquiring units of the financial
  institution include

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the person be resident in the particular
    province when the units are acquired, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that the units are required to be sold,
    transferred or redeemed within a reasonable time after the person
    ceases to be resident in the particular province; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the financial institution's percentage for the
  particular province and for the taxation year in which the preceding
  fiscal year ends, or the percentage that would be the financial
  institution's percentage for the particular province and for that
  taxation year if the particular province were a participating
  province, is 90% or more.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
- 2018, c. 27, s. 57
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20130418.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- investment plan with
provincial series

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** Section 9 does not apply
in respect of a reporting period in a fiscal year of a financial
institution that is a stratified investment plan if each series of the
financial institution is a provincial series for the fiscal year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- pension and private
investment plans

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** Section 9 does not apply
in respect of a reporting period in a fiscal year that ends in a
taxation year of a financial institution that is a private investment
plan or a pension entity of a pension plan if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} throughout the preceding taxation year, less than 10%
  of the total number of plan members of the financial institution are
  resident in the participating provinces; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} throughout the preceding fiscal year, the following
  amount is less than \$100,000,000:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pension entity of a pension plan,
    part of which is a defined contribution pension plan and the
    remaining part of which is a defined benefits pension plan, the
    amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#667456 .Subparagraph}
    A + B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the total value of the assets of the defined contribution
        pension plan that are reasonably attributable to the plan
        members of the financial institution resident in the
        participating provinces, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the total value of the actuarial liabilities of the defined
        benefits pension plan that are reasonably attributable to the
        plan members of the financial institution resident in the
        participating provinces,
    :::

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pension entity of a defined
    benefits pension plan, other than a pension entity described in
    subparagraph (i), the amount that is the total value of the
    actuarial liabilities of the pension plan that are reasonably
    attributable to the plan members of the financial institution
    resident in the participating provinces, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount that is the total
    value of the assets of the private investment plan or pension plan
    that are reasonably attributable to the plan members of the
    financial institution resident in the participating provinces.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- qualifying small investment
plan

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  investment plan is, or reasonably expects to be, a qualifying small
  investment plan for a fiscal year of the investment plan, if section
  13 does not apply in respect of a reporting period in the fiscal year
  and if no application by the investment plan under subsection 15(1) in
  respect of the fiscal year has been approved by the Minister, the
  investment plan may make an election to be a prescribed financial
  institution for the purpose of paragraph 225.2(1)(b) of the Act that
  is effective from the first day of the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph 225.2(1)(b) of the Act,
  if an election made under subsection (1) by an investment plan is in
  effect throughout a reporting period of the investment plan, the
  investment plan is a prescribed financial institution throughout the
  reporting period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of election

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An election made under subsection (1) by an
  investment plan is to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out the first fiscal year of the investment
    plan during which the election is to be in effect; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be filed with the Minister in prescribed manner on
    or before the first day of that first fiscal year or any later day
    that the Minister may allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An election made under subsection (1) by a person
  ceases to have effect on the day that is the earliest of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the first day of a fiscal year that ends in the
    first taxation year of the person for which the person does not meet
    the requirement set out in paragraph 9(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the first day of the fiscal year of the person in
    which the person ceases to be an investment plan; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a revocation of the election
    becomes effective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An investment plan that has made an election under
  subsection (1) may revoke the election, effective on the first day of
  a fiscal year of the investment plan that begins at least three years
  after the election became effective, or on the first day of any
  earlier fiscal year as the Minister may allow on application by the
  investment plan, by filing in prescribed manner a notice of revocation
  with the Minister in prescribed form containing prescribed information
  on or before the day on which the revocation is to become effective or
  any later day that the Minister may allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of early revocation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister allows an investment plan to revoke
  an election made under subsection (1) on the first day of a fiscal
  year that begins less than three years after the election became
  effective and the investment plan is a qualifying small investment
  plan for the fiscal year, section 9 does not apply in respect of any
  reporting period in the fiscal year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for small investment plan
status

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  investment plan may apply to the Minister to not have section 9 apply
  in respect of any reporting period in a particular fiscal year of the
  investment plan and in respect of any reporting period in the fiscal
  year of the investment plan following the particular fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an application made by an investment
  plan under subsection (1) in respect of a particular fiscal year of
  the investment plan and the fiscal year of the investment plan
  following the particular fiscal year, the Minister must, within 90
  days of that receipt, consider the application and, if it is
  reasonable, based on the information in the possession of the
  Minister, to expect that the investment plan will be a qualifying
  small investment plan for those two fiscal years, approve the
  application or, in any other case, refuse the application, and must,
  within that time limit, notify the investment plan in writing of the
  decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister approves an application made by an
  investment plan under subsection (1) in respect of a particular fiscal
  year of the investment plan and the fiscal year of the investment plan
  following the particular fiscal year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the investment plan is a qualifying small
    investment plan for the particular fiscal year, section 9 does not
    apply in respect of any reporting period in the particular fiscal
    year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the investment plan is a qualifying small
    investment plan for the following fiscal year, section 9 does not
    apply in respect of any reporting period in the following fiscal
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner of application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An application made by an investment plan under
  subsection (1) is to be made in prescribed form containing prescribed
  information and is to be filed with the Minister in prescribed manner
  on or before the particular day that is 90 days before the first day
  of the first fiscal year to which the application applies or on or
  before any day after the particular day that the Minister may allow.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-162, s. 12(F)
- SOR/2008-238, s. 1
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
:::

[Previous
Version](section-15-20080728.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Percentage for a Participating Province]{.HTitleText1} {#h-667498 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-667499 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[attribution point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[attribution point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
      particular series of a stratified investment plan, or in respect
      of a particular investment plan other than a stratified investment
      plan, and for a taxation year in which a fiscal year of the
      stratified investment plan or the particular investment plan, as
      the case may be, ends,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a particular series,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the particular series is an
          exchange-traded series, each of September 30 of the calendar
          year (in this definition referred to as the "particular
          calendar year") in which the fiscal year ends and

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} one or more of March 31, June 30 and
            December 31 of the particular calendar year, as determined
            by the stratified investment plan, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} March 31 of the particular calendar year,
            in the absence of such a determination by the stratified
            investment plan, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, September 30 of the
          particular calendar year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a particular investment plan,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is a
          distributed investment plan other than an exchange-traded
          fund, September 30 of the particular calendar year,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is an
          exchange-traded fund, each of September 30 of the particular
          calendar year and

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} one or more of March 31, June 30 and
            December 31 of the particular calendar year, as determined
            by the particular investment plan, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} March 31 of the particular calendar year,
            in the absence of such a determination by the particular
            investment plan, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is a
          pension entity of a defined benefits pension plan, the day
          that is the last day for which calculations of the actuarial
          liabilities of the plan have been completed and that is in the
          period that includes the particular calendar year and the
          three preceding calendar years or, if no such day exists,
          September 30 of the particular calendar year, and

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is a
          pension entity of a defined contribution pension plan or if
          the particular investment plan is not described in
          subparagraphs (i) to (iii), the day that is the last day for
          which the particular investment plan has, or can reasonably be
          expected to have, all or substantially all of the data
          required to calculate the particular investment plan's
          percentage for each participating province and for the
          taxation year and that is in the period that includes the
          particular calendar year and the preceding calendar year or,
          if no such day exists, September 30 of the particular calendar
          year. ([moment d'attribution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gross revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gross revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a selected listed
      financial institution for a particular period means the amount
      that would be the gross revenue of the financial institution for
      the particular period for the purposes of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if the financial institution were a taxpayer
      under that Act and if every reference in that Act to a taxation
      year of the financial institution were read as a reference to the
      particular period. ([revenu brut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[particular period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[particular period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in applying this Part for the purpose of the
        description of C in subsection 225.2(2) of the Act (other than
        for the determination of the amount for C in that subsection for
        the purpose of subsection 228(2.2) of the Act) and for the
        purpose of the description of A~6~ in subsection 225.2(2) of the
        Act, as adapted by subsection 48(1), a taxation year;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in applying this Part for the determination of
        the amount for C in subsection 225.2(2) of the Act for the
        purpose of subsection 228(2.2) of the Act, a reporting period;
        and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in applying this Part for the purpose of the
        description of D in subparagraph 237(5)(b)(ii) of the Act, a
        fiscal quarter. ([période donnée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[plan merger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plan merger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the merger or
      combination of two or more trusts, corporations or partnerships,
      each of which was, immediately before the merger or combination, a
      distributed investment plan and each of which is referred to in
      this definition as a "predecessor", to form one trust, corporation
      or partnership (referred to in this definition as the "continuing
      plan") in such a manner that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the continuing plan is a predecessor and is,
        immediately after the merger or combination, a distributed
        investment plan;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each predecessor other than the continuing
        plan, all or substantially all of the outstanding units of the
        predecessor are converted, by any means, into units of the
        continuing plan or are cancelled; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the merger or combination is otherwise than as
        a result of the acquisition of property of a particular trust,
        corporation or partnership by another trust, corporation or
        partnership, pursuant to the purchase of that property by the
        other trust, corporation or partnership or as a result of the
        distribution of that property to the other trust, corporation or
        partnership on the winding-up of the particular trust,
        corporation or partnership. ([fusion de
        régimes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified investor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified investor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in a particular
      distributed investment plan for a fiscal year of the particular
      investment plan that ends in a calendar year means a person (other
      than an individual or a distributed investment plan) that holds
      units of the particular investment plan as of September 30 of the
      calendar year and that meets the following criteria:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is an investment plan,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person holds units of the particular
          investment plan with a total value of less than \$10,000,000
          as of September 30 of the calendar year,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on or before December 31 of the calendar
          year, the person has not notified the particular investment
          plan that the person is a qualifying investor (as defined in
          subsection 52(1)) in the particular investment plan for the
          calendar year, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the particular investment plan neither
          knows nor ought to know that the person is a qualifying
          investor (as defined in subsection 52(1)) in the particular
          investment plan for the calendar year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, as of September 30 of the
        calendar year,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is a
          stratified investment plan, for each series of the particular
          investment plan in which the person holds units, the person
          holds units of the series with a total value of less than
          \$10,000,000, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the particular investment plan is a
          non-stratified investment plan, the person holds units of the
          particular investment plan with a total value of less than
          \$10,000,000. ([investisseur déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

  [[specified transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an attribution point in respect
        of a non-stratified investment plan for a taxation year of the
        investment plan, the acquisition of units of the investment plan
        by a person, or by a group of persons, from the investment plan
        if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the person, or each
          acquisition by a member of the group of persons, occurs less
          than 31 days before the attribution point,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the units are disposed of, within the
          meaning of subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
          Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), by the person, or by each member of the
          group of persons, within 30 days after the attribution point,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the acquisition of the units
          by a group of persons, each member of the group is related to
          every other member of the group,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total value of the units as of the
          attribution point is greater than the lesser of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} \$10,000,000, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 10% of the total value of all of the units
            of the investment plan on the attribution point,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the investment plan's percentage for any
          participating province and for the taxation year, determined
          without reference to subsection 32(3), is less than the amount
          that would be that percentage if that percentage were
          determined without reference to the units, and

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the person, or any
          acquisition by a member of the group of persons, does not meet
          one or more of the following conditions:

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition is undertaken by the person
            or member and the investment plan in good faith as part of
            the normal business practice of the investment plan,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the person or member and the investment
            plan deal with each other at arm's length,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition is made for consideration
            equal to or greater than the total value of the units at the
            time of the acquisition,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} neither the investment plan nor the manager
            of the investment plan provide any guarantees or indemnities
            to the person or member with respect to gains or losses in
            the value of the units during the period beginning on the
            particular day the acquisition occurred and ending on the
            day that is 30 days after the particular day, and

          - [(E)]{.lawlabel} any fees charged by the investment plan to
            the person or member in respect of the units are similar to
            fees charged by the investment plan to other persons holding
            units of the investment plan; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an attribution point in respect
        of a series of a stratified investment plan for a taxation year
        of the investment plan, the acquisition of units of the series
        by a person, or by a group of persons, from the investment plan
        if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the person, or each
          acquisition by a member of the group of persons, occurs less
          than 31 days before the attribution point,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the units are disposed of, within the
          meaning of subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
          Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), by the person, or by each member of the
          group of persons, within 30 days after the attribution point,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the acquisition of the units
          by a group of persons, each member of the group is related to
          every other member of the group,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total value of the units as of the
          attribution point is greater than the lesser of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} \$10,000,000, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 10% of the total value of all of the units
            of the series on the attribution point,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the investment plan's percentage for the
          series, for any participating province and for the taxation
          year, determined without reference to subsection 30(3), is
          less than the amount that would be that percentage if that
          percentage were determined without reference to the units, and

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the person, or any
          acquisition by a member of the group of persons, does not meet
          one or more of the following conditions:

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition is undertaken by the person
            or member and the investment plan in good faith as part of
            the normal business practice of the investment plan,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the person or member and the investment
            plan deal with each other at arm's length,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition is made for consideration
            equal to or greater than the total value of the units at the
            time of the acquisition,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} neither the investment plan nor the manager
            of the investment plan provide any guarantees or indemnities
            to the person or member with respect to gains or losses in
            the value of the units during the period beginning on the
            particular day the acquisition occurred and ending on the
            day that is 30 days after the particular day, and

          - [(E)]{.lawlabel} any fees charged by the investment plan to
            the person or member in respect of the units are similar to
            fees charged by the investment plan to other persons holding
            units of the series. ([opération
            déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[total gross revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[total gross revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a selected listed
      financial institution for a particular period means the portion of
      the gross revenue of the financial institution for the particular
      period that is reasonably attributable to the permanent
      establishments of the financial institution in Canada. ([revenu
      brut total]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to individual

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 22, 23 and 27, a
  reference to an individual includes a reference to a trust that is not
  an investment plan.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 2
- 2018, c. 27, s. 58
:::
